# Tasks

## TASKS.md Write Queue

To edit this file (mark tasks, update queues, add tasks), join this queue first. Only the name at the top may edit.


## Data Write Queue

Only the RA at the top of this list has write access to project data files (crosswalk JSON, CSVs). Add yourself to the bottom when you're ready to write. Remove yourself when done.

| 506 | Find and merge duplicate canonicals using shared-word matching. Write a Python script that: (1) Loads all canonical names from `2_webapp/org_clusters_crosswalk.json`. (4) Finds pairs of canonicals sharing 4+ significant words — these are merge candidates.
